        Idea:
Implement Phase 8 (Inbox surface) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 8).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 8 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `runs/<run-id>/inbox/` directory for external completion-signal protocol.
- Files dropped into the inbox by external processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal that an attested step has completed.
- Inbox files validate into events on the next `next`/`status`/`ack` call.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, lifecycle status/next handlers.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Inbox files validate into events.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Define inbox file format. A simple JSON shape:
   ```json
   {
     "step_id": "...",
     "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
     "evidence": { ... step-specific artifacts ... },
     "submitted_at": "ISO 8601",
     "submitted_by": "<external-system-name>"
   }
   ```

2. New module `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`:
   - `inbox_dir(run_dir) -> Path` — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`
   - `scan_inbox(run_dir) -> list[InboxEntry]` — reads, validates, returns parsed entries. Malformed files are logged and skipped, not raised.
   - `consume_inbox_entry(run_dir, entry) -> EventRecord` — validates the entry against the current step expectations, writes a hash-chained event, then moves the inbox file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<hash>` (or deletes — pick one and document it).

3. Hook into lifecycle handlers:
   - `artagents next`: scans inbox before computing next step. If the inbox has a valid entry for the current attested step, consume it (record event), then advance the cursor.
   - `artagents status`: scans inbox in read-only mode and surfaces "X inbox entries pending" in output.
   - `artagents ack`: not changed (ack is the explicit verb; inbox is the implicit signal channel).

4. Tests:
   - `tests/test_inbox_scan.py`: drops 3 valid + 1 malformed file, scan returns 3 valid entries and ignores the malformed one without raising.
   - `tests/test_inbox_consume.py`: full flow — attested step waits, drop a valid inbox entry, call `next`, assert event is recorded and cursor advances.
   - `tests/test_inbox_stale.py`: drop an entry referencing a step_id that doesn't match current step, assert it's ignored (logged, not consumed).

5. `runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md` template should mention the inbox surface for human/external operators.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 8 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies.
- Inbox is opt-in: presence of `inbox/` directory triggers scanning, absence skips it.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 8 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new inbox tests + status/next surface inbox entries appropriately.

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-8-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high/artagents/core/task/inbox.py\n+\"\"\"External completion-signal inbox for task runs.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+import hashlib\n+import json\n+import logging\n+from dataclasses import dataclass\n+from datetime import datetime\n+from pathlib import Path\n+from typing import Any, Literal, TypeAlias\n+\n+\n+LOGGER = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n+\n+InboxDecision: TypeAlias = Literal[\"approve\", \"retry\", \"abort\"]\n+EventRecord: TypeAlias = dict[str, Any]\n+\n+_VALID_DECISIONS: set[str] = {\"approve\", \"retry\", \"abort\"}\n+\n+\n+@dataclass(frozen=True)\n+class InboxEntry:\n+    step_id: str\n+    decision: InboxDecision\n+    evidence: dict[str, Any]\n+    submitted_at: str\n+    submitted_by: str\n+    source_path: Path\n+    source_hash: str\n+\n+\n+def inbox_dir(run_dir: str | Path) -> Path:\n+    \"\"\"Return the opt-in inbox directory for ``run_dir``.\"\"\"\n+\n+    return Path(run_dir) / \"inbox\"\n+\n+\n+def scan_inbox(run_dir: str | Path) -> list[InboxEntry]:\n+    \"\"\"Read and structurally validate inbox files.\n+\n+    Missing inbox directories are a no-op. Dotfiles, directories, and the\n+    reserved ``.consumed`` folder are ignored. Malformed inbox files are logged\n+    and skipped so external operators cannot crash lifecycle commands by\n+    dropping bad input.\n+    \"\"\"\n+\n+    root = inbox_dir(run_dir)\n+    if not root.is_dir():\n+        return []\n+\n+    entries: list[InboxEntry] = []\n+    for path in sorted(root.iterdir(), key=lambda item: item.name):\n+        if path.name.startswith(\".\") or not path.is_file():\n+            continue\n+        try:\n+            entry = _read_inbox_file(path)\n+        except ValueError as exc:\n+            LOGGER.warning(\"ignoring malformed inbox file %s: %s\", path, exc)\n+            continue\n+        entries.append(entry)\n+    return entries\n+\n+\n+def _read_inbox_file(path: Path) -> InboxEntry:\n+    try:\n+        raw = path.read_bytes()\n+    except OSError as exc:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"failed to read file: {exc}\") from exc\n+\n+    try:\n+        payload = json.loads(raw.decode(\"utf-8\"))\n+    except UnicodeDecodeError as exc:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"file is not UTF-8: {exc}\") from exc\n+    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"invalid JSON: {exc.msg}\") from exc\n+\n+    if not isinstance(payload, dict):\n+        raise ValueError(\"top-level payload must be an object\")\n+\n+    step_id = _required_string(payload, \"step_id\")\n+    decision = _required_string(payload, \"decision\")\n+    if decision not in _VALID_DECISIONS:\n+        raise ValueError(\"decision must be one of: approve, retry, abort\")\n+\n+    evidence = payload.get(\"evidence\")\n+    if not isinstance(evidence, dict):\n+        raise ValueError(\"evidence must be an object\")\n+\n+    submitted_at = _required_string(payload, \"submitted_at\")\n+    _validate_iso8601(submitted_at)\n+    submitted_by = _required_string(payload, \"submitted_by\")\n+\n+    return InboxEntry(\n+        step_id=step_id,\n+        decision=decision,  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n+        evidence=dict(evidence),\n+        submitted_at=submitted_at,\n+        submitted_by=submitted_by,\n+        source_path=path,\n+        source_hash=f\"sha256:{hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()}\",\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def _required_string(payload: dict[str, Any], key: str) -> str:\n+    value = payload.get(key)\n+    if not isinstance(value, str) or value == \"\":\n+        raise ValueError(f\"{key} must be a non-empty string\")\n+    return value\n+\n+\n+def _validate_iso8601(value: str) -> None:\n+    candidate = value\n+    if candidate.endswith(\"Z\"):\n+        candidate = f\"{candidate[:-1]}+00:00\"\n+    try:\n+        datetime.fromisoformat(candidate)\n+    except ValueError as exc:\n+        raise ValueError(\"submitted_at must be ISO 8601\") from exc\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-8-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 8 (Inbox surface) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 8).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 8 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `runs/<run-id>/inbox/` directory for external completion-signal protocol.
- Files dropped into the inbox by external processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal that an attested step has completed.
- Inbox files validate into events on the next `next`/`status`/`ack` call.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, lifecycle status/next handlers.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Inbox files validate into events.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Define inbox file format. A simple JSON shape:
   ```json
   {
     "step_id": "...",
     "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
     "evidence": { ... step-specific artifacts ... },
     "submitted_at": "ISO 8601",
     "submitted_by": "<external-system-name>"
   }
   ```

2. New module `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`:
   - `inbox_dir(run_dir) -> Path` — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`
   - `scan_inbox(run_dir) -> list[InboxEntry]` — reads, validates, returns parsed entries. Malformed files are logged and skipped, not raised.
   - `consume_inbox_entry(run_dir, entry) -> EventRecord` — validates the entry against the current step expectations, writes a hash-chained event, then moves the inbox file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<hash>` (or deletes — pick one and document it).

3. Hook into lifecycle handlers:
   - `artagents next`: scans inbox before computing next step. If the inbox has a valid entry for the current attested step, consume it (record event), then advance the cursor.
   - `artagents status`: scans inbox in read-only mode and surfaces "X inbox entries pending" in output.
   - `artagents ack`: not changed (ack is the explicit verb; inbox is the implicit signal channel).

4. Tests:
   - `tests/test_inbox_scan.py`: drops 3 valid + 1 malformed file, scan returns 3 valid entries and ignores the malformed one without raising.
   - `tests/test_inbox_consume.py`: full flow — attested step waits, drop a valid inbox entry, call `next`, assert event is recorded and cursor advances.
   - `tests/test_inbox_stale.py`: drop an entry referencing a step_id that doesn't match current step, assert it's ignored (logged, not consumed).

5. `runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md` template should mention the inbox surface for human/external operators.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 8 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies.
- Inbox is opt-in: presence of `inbox/` directory triggers scanning, absence skips it.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 8 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new inbox tests + status/next surface inbox entries appropriately.

        Idea:
Implement Phase 8 (Inbox surface) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 8).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 8 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `runs/<run-id>/inbox/` directory for external completion-signal protocol.
- Files dropped into the inbox by external processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal that an attested step has completed.
- Inbox files validate into events on the next `next`/`status`/`ack` call.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, lifecycle status/next handlers.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Inbox files validate into events.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Define inbox file format. A simple JSON shape:
   ```json
   {
     "step_id": "...",
     "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
     "evidence": { ... step-specific artifacts ... },
     "submitted_at": "ISO 8601",
     "submitted_by": "<external-system-name>"
   }
   ```

2. New module `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`:
   - `inbox_dir(run_dir) -> Path` — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`
   - `scan_inbox(run_dir) -> list[InboxEntry]` — reads, validates, returns parsed entries. Malformed files are logged and skipped, not raised.
   - `consume_inbox_entry(run_dir, entry) -> EventRecord` — validates the entry against the current step expectations, writes a hash-chained event, then moves the inbox file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<hash>` (or deletes — pick one and document it).

3. Hook into lifecycle handlers:
   - `artagents next`: scans inbox before computing next step. If the inbox has a valid entry for the current attested step, consume it (record event), then advance the cursor.
   - `artagents status`: scans inbox in read-only mode and surfaces "X inbox entries pending" in output.
   - `artagents ack`: not changed (ack is the explicit verb; inbox is the implicit signal channel).

4. Tests:
   - `tests/test_inbox_scan.py`: drops 3 valid + 1 malformed file, scan returns 3 valid entries and ignores the malformed one without raising.
   - `tests/test_inbox_consume.py`: full flow — attested step waits, drop a valid inbox entry, call `next`, assert event is recorded and cursor advances.
   - `tests/test_inbox_stale.py`: drop an entry referencing a step_id that doesn't match current step, assert it's ignored (logged, not consumed).

5. `runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md` template should mention the inbox surface for human/external operators.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 8 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies.
- Inbox is opt-in: presence of `inbox/` directory triggers scanning, absence skips it.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 8 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new inbox tests + status/next surface inbox entries appropriately.

      "executor_notes": "Added `actionable_inbox_entries(run_dir, entries=None)`. It derives project/run context from `run_dir`, reads and validates active run state, compares `plan.json` hash to the active-run pin, verifies `events.jsonl` with `verify_chain`, loads plan/events, and uses `peek_current_step`. It returns only `decision == \"approve\"` entries whose `step_id` equals the current attested cursor path. Retry, abort, stale/non-current, exhausted, inactive, non-attested, bad plan, and bad chain cases are logged and ignored without mutation. Full suite ran with 714 passed, 7 skipped, 2 xfailed, 3 unrelated environment failures.",
      "files_changed": [
        "artagents/core/task/inbox.py"
      ],
      "commands_run": [
        "python3 -m py_compile artagents/core/task/inbox.py",
        "python3 - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom tempfile import TemporaryDirectory\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport sys\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path('tests').resolve()))\nfrom _lifecycle_fixtures import setup_run\nfrom artagents.core.task.inbox import actionable_inbox_entries, inbox_dir, scan_inbox\n\nBODY = '''from artagents.orchestrate import orchestrator, attested, code\n@orchestrator(\"demo.inbox\")\ndef main(): return [\n    attested(\"review\", command=\"review.sh\", instructions=\"check\", ack=\"actor\"),\n    code(\"after\", argv=[\"echo\", \"after\"]),\n]\n'''\n\ndef write_entry(path, step_id, decision):\n    path.write_text(json.dumps({\n        'step_id': step_id,\n        'decision': decision,\n        'evidence': {'artifact': path.name},\n        'submitted_at': '2026-05-05T12:00:00Z',\n        'submitted_by': 'external-system',\n    }), encoding='utf-8')\n\nwith TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    tmp = Path(d)\n    _packs, projects = setup_run(tmp, 'demo', 'inbox', BODY, 'demo.inbox', run_id='r1')\n    run_dir = projects / 'p' / 'runs' / 'r1'\n    box = inbox_dir(run_dir)\n    box.mkdir()\n    write_entry(box / 'current.json', 'review', 'approve')\n    write_entry(box / 'retry.json', 'review', 'retry')\n    write_entry(box / 'abort.json', 'review', 'abort')\n    write_entry(box / 'stale.json', 'after', 'approve')\n    events_path = run_dir / 'events.jsonl'\n    before = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    before_files = sorted(p.name for p in box.iterdir())\n    actionable = actionable_inbox_entries(run_dir, scan_inbox(run_dir))\n    after = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    after_files = sorted(p.name for p in box.iterdir())\n    assert [entry.source_path.name for entry in actionable] == ['current.json']\n    assert before == after\n    assert before_files == after_files\nprint('ok')\nPY",
        "pytest tests/test_lifecycle_next.py tests/test_lifecycle_status.py tests/test_lifecycle_ack.py tests/test_lifecycle_peek.py tests/test_task_kernel_events.py",
        "pytest tests/"

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-8-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 8 (Inbox surface) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 8).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 8 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `runs/<run-id>/inbox/` directory for external completion-signal protocol.
- Files dropped into the inbox by external processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal that an attested step has completed.
- Inbox files validate into events on the next `next`/`status`/`ack` call.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, lifecycle status/next handlers.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Inbox files validate into events.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Define inbox file format. A simple JSON shape:
   ```json
   {
     "step_id": "...",
     "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
     "evidence": { ... step-specific artifacts ... },
     "submitted_at": "ISO 8601",
     "submitted_by": "<external-system-name>"
   }
   ```

2. New module `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`:
   - `inbox_dir(run_dir) -> Path` — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`
   - `scan_inbox(run_dir) -> list[InboxEntry]` — reads, validates, returns parsed entries. Malformed files are logged and skipped, not raised.
   - `consume_inbox_entry(run_dir, entry) -> EventRecord` — validates the entry against the current step expectations, writes a hash-chained event, then moves the inbox file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<hash>` (or deletes — pick one and document it).

3. Hook into lifecycle handlers:
   - `artagents next`: scans inbox before computing next step. If the inbox has a valid entry for the current attested step, consume it (record event), then advance the cursor.
   - `artagents status`: scans inbox in read-only mode and surfaces "X inbox entries pending" in output.
   - `artagents ack`: not changed (ack is the explicit verb; inbox is the implicit signal channel).

4. Tests:
   - `tests/test_inbox_scan.py`: drops 3 valid + 1 malformed file, scan returns 3 valid entries and ignores the malformed one without raising.
   - `tests/test_inbox_consume.py`: full flow — attested step waits, drop a valid inbox entry, call `next`, assert event is recorded and cursor advances.
   - `tests/test_inbox_stale.py`: drop an entry referencing a step_id that doesn't match current step, assert it's ignored (logged, not consumed).

After preflight it reruns the normal mutating `_auto_traverse_to_leaf` path so prerequisite traversal events persist, then appends `step_attested` or `item_attested` with `attestor_kind=\"inbox\"`, `attestor_id=submitted_by`, `evidence=[]`, and object metadata under `inbox_evidence`. It returns a normal attested `GateDecision` and runs existing inline produces checks. Smoke verification confirmed nested traversal event parity with normal gate dispatch, hash-chain validity, inbox metadata shape, and stale `step_id` rejection without changing `events.jsonl`. Focused gate/kernel tests passed: 44 passed. Full suite ran with 714 passed, 7 skipped, 2 xfailed, 3 unrelated environment failures.",

InboxEntry | None:\n+    entry_path = Path(entry.source_path)\n+    for candidate in scan_inbox(run_dir):\n+        if (\n+            _same_source_path(candidate.source_path, entry_path)\n+            and candidate.source_hash == entry.source_hash\n+        ):\n+            return candidate\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def _same_source_path(left: Path, right: Path) -> bool:\n+    try:\n+        return left.resolve(strict=False) == right.resolve(strict=False)\n+    except OSError:\n+        return left == right\n+\n+\n+def _find_written_inbox_event(\n+    events: Sequence[EventRecord],\n+    source_hash: str,\n+) -> EventRecord | None:\n+    for event in events:\n+        if event.get(\"kind\") not in {\"step_attested\", \"item_attested\"}:\n+            continue\n+        if event.get(\"attestor_kind\") != \"inbox\":\n+            continue\n+        inbox_evidence = event.get(\"inbox_evidence\")\n+        if not isinstance(inbox_evidence, dict):\n+            continue\n+        if inbox_evidence.get(\"inbox_source_hash\") == source_hash:\n+            return event\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def _consumed_path(run_dir: Path, source_hash: str) -> Path:\n+    digest = source_hash.split(\":\", 1)[-1]\n+    consumed_dir = inbox_dir(run_dir) / \".consumed\"\n+    candidate = consumed_dir / f\"{digest}.json\"\n+    if not candidate.exists():\n+        return candidate\n+    suffix = 1\n+    while True:\n+        candidate = consumed_dir / f\"{digest}-{suffix}.json\"\n+        if not candidate.exists():\n+            return candidate\n+        suffix += 1\n+\n+\n+def _run_identity(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[str, Path]:\n+    _run_id, slug, _project_root, projects_root = _run_context(run_dir)\n+    return slug, projects_root\n+\n+\n+def _run_context(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[str, str, Path, Path]:\n+    run_id = run_dir.name\n+    project_root = run_dir.parent.parent\n+    slug = project_root.name\n+    projects_root = project_root.parent\n+    return run_id, slug, project_root, projects_root\n*** End Patch\n"

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-8-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 8 (Inbox surface) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 8).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 8 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `runs/<run-id>/inbox/` directory for external completion-signal protocol.
- Files dropped into the inbox by external processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal that an attested step has completed.
- Inbox files validate into events on the next `next`/`status`/`ack` call.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, lifecycle status/next handlers.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Inbox files validate into events.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Define inbox file format. A simple JSON shape:
   ```json
   {
     "step_id": "...",
     "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
     "evidence": { ... step-specific artifacts ... },
     "submitted_at": "ISO 8601",
     "submitted_by": "<external-system-name>"
   }
   ```

2. New module `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`:
   - `inbox_dir(run_dir) -> Path` — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`
   - `scan_inbox(run_dir) -> list[InboxEntry]` — reads, validates, returns parsed entries. Malformed files are logged and skipped, not raised.
   - `consume_inbox_entry(run_dir, entry) -> EventRecord` — validates the entry against the current step expectations, writes a hash-chained event, then moves the inbox file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<hash>` (or deletes — pick one and document it).

3. Hook into lifecycle handlers:
   - `artagents next`: scans inbox before computing next step. If the inbox has a valid entry for the current attested step, consume it (record event), then advance the cursor.
   - `artagents status`: scans inbox in read-only mode and surfaces "X inbox entries pending" in output.
   - `artagents ack`: not changed (ack is the explicit verb; inbox is the implicit signal channel).

4. Tests:
   - `tests/test_inbox_scan.py`: drops 3 valid + 1 malformed file, scan returns 3 valid entries and ignores the malformed one without raising.
   - `tests/test_inbox_consume.py`: full flow — attested step waits, drop a valid inbox entry, call `next`, assert event is recorded and cursor advances.
   - `tests/test_inbox_stale.py`: drop an entry referencing a step_id that doesn't match current step, assert it's ignored (logged, not consumed).

5. `runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md` template should mention the inbox surface for human/external operators.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 8 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies.
- Inbox is opt-in: presence of `inbox/` directory triggers scanning, absence skips it.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 8 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new inbox tests + status/next surface inbox entries appropriately.

      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "Added `actionable_inbox_entries(run_dir, entries=None)`. It derives project/run context from `run_dir`, reads and validates active run state, compares `plan.json` hash to the active-run pin, verifies `events.jsonl` with `verify_chain`, loads plan/events, and uses `peek_current_step`. Full suite ran with 714 passed, 7 skipped, 2 xfailed, 3 unrelated environment failures.",
      "files_changed": [
        "artagents/core/task/inbox.py"
      ],
      "commands_run": [
        "python3 -m py_compile artagents/core/task/inbox.py",
        "python3 - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom tempfile import TemporaryDirectory\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport sys\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path('tests').resolve()))\nfrom _lifecycle_fixtures import setup_run\nfrom artagents.core.task.inbox import actionable_inbox_entries, inbox_dir, scan_inbox\n\nBODY = '''from artagents.orchestrate import orchestrator, attested, code\n@orchestrator(\"demo.inbox\")\ndef main(): return [\n    attested(\"review\", command=\"review.sh\", instructions=\"check\", ack=\"actor\"),\n    code(\"after\", argv=[\"echo\", \"after\"]),\n]\n'''\n\ndef write_entry(path, step_id, decision):\n    path.write_text(json.dumps({\n        'step_id': step_id,\n        'decision': decision,\n        'evidence': {'artifact': path.name},\n        'submitted_at': '2026-05-05T12:00:00Z',\n        'submitted_by': 'external-system',\n    }), encoding='utf-8')\n\nwith TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    tmp = Path(d)\n    _packs, projects = setup_run(tmp, 'demo', 'inbox', BODY, 'demo.inbox', run_id='r1')\n    run_dir = projects / 'p' / 'runs' / 'r1'\n    box = inbox_dir(run_dir)\n    box.mkdir()\n    write_entry(box / 'current.json', 'review', 'approve')\n    write_entry(box / 'retry.json', 'review', 'retry')\n    write_entry(box / 'abort.json', 'review', 'abort')\n    write_entry(box / 'stale.json', 'after', 'approve')\n    events_path = run_dir / 'events.jsonl'\n    before = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    before_files = sorted(p.name for p in box.iterdir())\n    actionable = actionable_inbox_entries(run_dir, scan_inbox(run_dir))\n    after = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    after_files = sorted(p.name for p in box.iterdir())\n    assert [entry.source_path.name for entry in actionable] == ['current.json']\n    assert before == after\n    assert before_files == after_files\nprint('ok')\nPY",
        "pytest tests/test_lifecycle_next.py tests/test_lifecycle_status.py tests/test_lifecycle_ack.py tests/test_lifecycle_peek.py tests/test_task_kernel_events.py",
        "pytest tests/"

        "python3 - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom tempfile import TemporaryDirectory\nimport hashlib\nimport json\n\nfrom artagents.core.project.project import create_project\nfrom artagents.core.task.active_run import write_active_run\nfrom artagents.core.task.events import append_event, make_run_started_event\nfrom artagents.core.task.gate import TaskRunGateError, gate_external_attestation\nfrom artagents.core.task.plan import compute_plan_hash\n\nPLAN = {\n    'plan_id': 'p1',\n    'version': 1,\n    'steps': [{\n        'id': 'review',\n        'kind': 'attested',\n        'command': 'ack --project demo --step review',\n        'instructions': 'Review',\n        'ack': {'kind': 'actor'},\n    }],\n}\nwith TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    root = Path(d)\n    create_project('demo', root=root)\n    plan_path = root / 'demo' / 'plan.json'\n    plan_path.write_text(json.dumps(PLAN), encoding='utf-8')\n    plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(plan_path)\n    write_active_run('demo', run_id='run-1', plan_hash=plan_hash, root=root)\n    events_path = root / 'demo' / 'runs' / 'run-1' / 'events.jsonl'\n    events_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    append_event(events_path, make_run_started_event('run-1', plan_hash, actor='starter'))\n    before = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    try:\n        gate_external_attestation(\n            'demo',\n            'other',\n            submitted_by='external-system',\n            evidence={},\n            submitted_at='2026-05-05T12:00:00Z',\n            inbox_source_hash='sha256:' + 'b' * 64,\n            root=root,\n        )\n    except TaskRunGateError:\n        pass\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError('expected stale step_id rejection')\n    after = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    assert before == after\nprint('ok')\nPY",

 with redirect_stdout(next_out), redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):\n        rc = cmd_next(['--project', 'p'], projects_root=projects)\n    assert rc == 0\n    assert 'run: echo after' in  assert len(consumed) == 1\n    remaining = sorted(p.name for p in box.iterdir() if p.is_file())\n    assert remaining == ['b.json']\n\n    status_after = io.StringIO()\n    with         Idea:
Implement Phase 8 (Inbox surface) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 8).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 8 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `runs/<run-id>/inbox/` directory for external completion-signal protocol.
- Files dropped into the inbox by external processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal that an attested step has completed.
- Inbox files validate into events on the next `next`/`status`/`ack` call.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, lifecycle status/next handlers.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Inbox files validate into events.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Define inbox file format. A simple JSON shape:
   ```json
   {
     "step_id": "...",
     "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
     "evidence": { ... step-specific artifacts ... },
     "submitted_at": "ISO 8601",
     "submitted_by": "<external-system-name>"
   }
   ```

2. New module `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`:
   - `inbox_dir(run_dir) -> Path` — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`
   - `scan_inbox(run_dir) -> list[InboxEntry]` — reads, validates, returns parsed entries. Malformed files are logged and skipped, not raised.
   - `consume_inbox_entry(run_dir, entry) -> EventRecord` — validates the entry against the current step expectations, writes a hash-chained event, then moves the inbox file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<hash>` (or deletes — pick one and document it).

3. Hook into lifecycle handlers:
   - `artagents next`: scans inbox before computing next step. If the inbox has a valid entry for the current attested step, consume it (record event), then advance the cursor.
   - `artagents status`: scans inbox in read-only mode and surfaces "X inbox entries pending" in output.
   - `artagents ack`: not changed (ack is the explicit verb; inbox is the implicit signal channel).

4. Tests:
   - `tests/test_inbox_scan.py`: drops 3 valid + 1 malformed file, scan returns 3 valid entries and ignores the malformed one without raising.
   - `tests/test_inbox_consume.py`: full flow — attested step waits, drop a valid inbox entry, call `next`, assert event is recorded and cursor advances.
   - `tests/test_inbox_stale.py`: drop an entry referencing a step_id that doesn't match current step, assert it's ignored (logged, not consumed).

5. `runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md` template should mention the inbox surface for human/external operators.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 8 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies.
- Inbox is opt-in: presence of `inbox/` directory triggers scanning, absence skips it.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 8 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new inbox tests + status/next surface inbox entries appropriately.

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-8-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 8 (Inbox surface) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 8).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 8 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `runs/<run-id>/inbox/` directory for external completion-signal protocol.
- Files dropped into the inbox by external processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal that an attested step has completed.
- Inbox files validate into events on the next `next`/`status`/`ack` call.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, lifecycle status/next handlers.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Inbox files validate into events.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Define inbox file format. A simple JSON shape:
   ```json
   {
     "step_id": "...",
     "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
     "evidence": { ... step-specific artifacts ... },
     "submitted_at": "ISO 8601",
     "submitted_by": "<external-system-name>"
   }
   ```

2. New module `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`:
   - `inbox_dir(run_dir) -> Path` — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`
   - `scan_inbox(run_dir) -> list[InboxEntry]` — reads, validates, returns parsed entries. Malformed files are logged and skipped, not raised.
   - `consume_inbox_entry(run_dir, entry) -> EventRecord` — validates the entry against the current step expectations, writes a hash-chained event, then moves the inbox file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<hash>` (or deletes — pick one and document it).

3. Hook into lifecycle handlers:
   - `artagents next`: scans inbox before computing next step. If the inbox has a valid entry for the current attested step, consume it (record event), then advance the cursor.
   - `artagents status`: scans inbox in read-only mode and surfaces "X inbox entries pending" in output.
   - `artagents ack`: not changed (ack is the explicit verb; inbox is the implicit signal channel).

4. Tests:
   - `tests/test_inbox_scan.py`: drops 3 valid + 1 malformed file, scan returns 3 valid entries and ignores the malformed one without raising.
   - `tests/test_inbox_consume.py`: full flow — attested step waits, drop a valid inbox entry, call `next`, assert event is recorded and cursor advances.
   - `tests/test_inbox_stale.py`: drop an entry referencing a step_id that doesn't match current step, assert it's ignored (logged, not consumed).

5. `runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md` template should mention the inbox surface for human/external operators.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 8 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies.
- Inbox is opt-in: presence of `inbox/` directory triggers scanning, absence skips it.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 8 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new inbox tests + status/next surface inbox entries appropriately.

      "commands_run": [
        "python3 -m py_compile artagents/core/task/inbox.py",
        "python3 - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom tempfile import TemporaryDirectory\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport sys\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path('tests').resolve()))\nfrom _lifecycle_fixtures import setup_run\nfrom artagents.core.task.inbox import actionable_inbox_entries, inbox_dir, scan_inbox\n\nBODY = '''from artagents.orchestrate import orchestrator, attested, code\n@orchestrator(\"demo.inbox\")\ndef main(): return [\n    attested(\"review\", command=\"review.sh\", instructions=\"check\", ack=\"actor\"),\n    code(\"after\", argv=[\"echo\", \"after\"]),\n]\n'''\n\ndef write_entry(path, step_id, decision):\n    path.write_text(json.dumps({\n        'step_id': step_id,\n        'decision': decision,\n        'evidence': {'artifact': path.name},\n        'submitted_at': '2026-05-05T12:00:00Z',\n        'submitted_by': 'external-system',\n    }), encoding='utf-8')\n\nwith TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    tmp = Path(d)\n    _packs, projects = setup_run(tmp, 'demo', 'inbox', BODY, 'demo.inbox', run_id='r1')\n    run_dir = projects / 'p' / 'runs' / 'r1'\n    box = inbox_dir(run_dir)\n    box.mkdir()\n    write_entry(box / 'current.json', 'review', 'approve')\n    write_entry(box / 'retry.json', 'review', 'retry')\n    write_entry(box / 'abort.json', 'review', 'abort')\n    write_entry(box / 'stale.json', 'after', 'approve')\n    events_path = run_dir / 'events.jsonl'\n    before = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    before_files = sorted(p.name for p in box.iterdir())\n    actionable = actionable_inbox_entries(run_dir, scan_inbox(run_dir))\n    after = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    after_files = sorted(p.name for p in box.iterdir())\n    assert [entry.source_path.name for entry in actionable] == ['current.json']\n    assert before == after\n    assert before_files == after_files\nprint('ok')\nPY",

        "python3 - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom tempfile import TemporaryDirectory\nimport hashlib\nimport json\n\nfrom artagents.core.project.project import create_project\nfrom artagents.core.task.active_run import write_active_run\nfrom artagents.core.task.events import append_event, make_run_started_event\nfrom artagents.core.task.gate import TaskRunGateError, gate_external_attestation\nfrom artagents.core.task.plan import compute_plan_hash\n\nPLAN = {\n    'plan_id': 'p1',\n    'version': 1,\n    'steps': [{\n        'id': 'review',\n        'kind': 'attested',\n        'command': 'ack --project demo --step review',\n        'instructions': 'Review',\n        'ack': {'kind': 'actor'},\n    }],\n}\nwith TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    root = Path(d)\n    create_project('demo', root=root)\n    plan_path = root / 'demo' / 'plan.json'\n    plan_path.write_text(json.dumps(PLAN), encoding='utf-8')\n    plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(plan_path)\n    write_active_run('demo', run_id='run-1', plan_hash=plan_hash, root=root)\n    events_path = root / 'demo' / 'runs' / 'run-1' / 'events.jsonl'\n    events_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    append_event(events_path, make_run_started_event('run-1', plan_hash, actor='starter'))\n    before = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    try:\n        gate_external_attestation(\n            'demo',\n            'other',\n            submitted_by='external-system',\n            evidence={},\n            submitted_at='2026-05-05T12:00:00Z',\n            inbox_source_hash='sha256:' + 'b' * 64,\n            root=root,\n        )\n    except TaskRunGateError:\n        pass\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError('expected stale step_id rejection')\n    after = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    assert before == after\nprint('ok')\nPY",

path.write_text(json.dumps({\n        'step_id': 'review',\n        'decision': 'approve',\n        'evidence': {'artifact': artifact},\n        'submitted_at': '2026-05-05T12:00:00Z',\n artagents.core.task.inbox import inbox_dir, scan_inbox\n+\n+\n+def _write_entry(path: Path, *, step_id: str, decision: str) -> None:\n+    path.write_text(\n+        json.dumps(\n+            {\n+                \"step_id\": step_id,\n+                \"decision\": decision,\n+                \"evidence\": {\"artifact\": path.name},\n+                \"submitted_at\": \"2026-05-05T12:00:00Z\",\n+         Idea:
Implement Phase 8 (Inbox surface) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 8).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 8 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `runs/<run-id>/inbox/` directory for external completion-signal protocol.
- Files dropped into the inbox by external processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal that an attested step has completed.
- Inbox files validate into events on the next `next`/`status`/`ack` call.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, lifecycle status/next handlers.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Inbox files validate into events.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Define inbox file format. A simple JSON shape:
   ```json
   {
     "step_id": "...",
     "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
     "evidence": { ... step-specific artifacts ... },
     "submitted_at": "ISO 8601",
     "submitted_by": "<external-system-name>"
   }
   ```

2. New module `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`:
   - `inbox_dir(run_dir) -> Path` — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`
   - `scan_inbox(run_dir) -> list[InboxEntry]` — reads, validates, returns parsed entries. Malformed files are logged and skipped, not raised.
   - `consume_inbox_entry(run_dir, entry) -> EventRecord` — validates the entry against the current step expectations, writes a hash-chained event, then moves the inbox file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<hash>` (or deletes — pick one and document it).

3. Hook into lifecycle handlers:
   - `artagents next`: scans inbox before computing next step. If the inbox has a valid entry for the current attested step, consume it (record event), then advance the cursor.
   - `artagents status`: scans inbox in read-only mode and surfaces "X inbox entries pending" in output.
   - `artagents ack`: not changed (ack is the explicit verb; inbox is the implicit signal channel).

4. Tests:
   - `tests/test_inbox_scan.py`: drops 3 valid + 1 malformed file, scan returns 3 valid entries and ignores the malformed one without raising.
   - `tests/test_inbox_consume.py`: full flow — attested step waits, drop a valid inbox entry, call `next`, assert event is recorded and cursor advances.
   - `tests/test_inbox_stale.py`: drop an entry referencing a step_id that doesn't match current step, assert it's ignored (logged, not consumed).

5. `runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md` template should mention the inbox surface for human/external operators.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 8 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies.
- Inbox is opt-in: presence of `inbox/` directory triggers scanning, absence skips it.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 8 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new inbox tests + status/next surface inbox entries appropriately.

        "python3 - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom tempfile import TemporaryDirectory\nimport hashlib\nimport json\n\nfrom artagents.core.project.project import create_project\nfrom artagents.core.task.active_run import write_active_run\nfrom artagents.core.task.events import append_event, make_run_started_event\nfrom artagents.core.task.gate import TaskRunGateError, gate_external_attestation\nfrom artagents.core.task.plan import compute_plan_hash\n\nPLAN = {\n    'plan_id': 'p1',\n    'version': 1,\n    'steps': [{\n        'id': 'review',\n        'kind': 'attested',\n        'command': 'ack --project demo --step review',\n        'instructions': 'Review',\n        'ack': {'kind': 'actor'},\n    }],\n}\nwith TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    root = Path(d)\n    create_project('demo', root=root)\n    plan_path = root / 'demo' / 'plan.json'\n    plan_path.write_text(json.dumps(PLAN), encoding='utf-8')\n    plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(plan_path)\n    write_active_run('demo', run_id='run-1', plan_hash=plan_hash, root=root)\n    events_path = root / 'demo' / 'runs' / 'run-1' / 'events.jsonl'\n    events_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    append_event(events_path, make_run_started_event('run-1', plan_hash, actor='starter'))\n    before = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    try:\n        gate_external_attestation(\n            'demo',\n            'other',\n            submitted_by='external-system',\n            evidence={},\n            submitted_at='2026-05-05T12:00:00Z',\n            inbox_source_hash='sha256:' + 'b' * 64,\n            root=root,\n        )\n    except TaskRunGateError:\n        pass\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError('expected stale step_id rejection')\n    after = hashlib.sha256(events_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    assert before == after\nprint('ok')\nPY",
